<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_016wimNMjXhUGqV79rtEv8MZ`</summary>

```text
1	# Trace-Verdict-Study 文献调研报告
2	
3	日期：2026-05-15
4	
5	---
6	
7	## 方向 1: Trace SFT / Trace-augmented LLM Training 全景
8	
9	除已知工作外，找到 8 篇直接相关工作：
10	
11	| # | 标题 | 会议/年份 | 核心做法 | 与我们的关系 |
12	|---|------|----------|----------|-------------|
13	| 1 | **SemCoder: Training Code Language Models with Comprehensive Semantics Reasoning** | NeurIPS 2024 | 训练 Code LLM 用自然语言逐行描述控制流和变量状态（"execution monologue"），在全量可执行 Python 上做 SFT | trace-as-text SFT 的代表性工作，关注"模拟执行"而非分类 |
14	| 2 | **NExT: Teaching LLMs to Reason about Code Execution** | arXiv 2024 (Google DeepMind) | 自训练方法，将行级变量状态 trace 嵌入 CoT，SFT 微调 PaLM 2 做程序修复 | trace SFT 用于 repair，证明 trace-aware CoT 可泛化到推理时无 trace 场景 |
15	| 3 | **CodeExecutor** | Findings of ACL 2023 | 构造 mutation-based Python 数据集（含逐行变量状态），课程学习做 execution trace pretraining | 最早的 trace pretraining 工作之一，让模型学会"执行"代码 |
16	| 4 | **What I Cannot Execute, I Do Not Understand (E.T.)** | arXiv 2025 (Meta + Edinburgh) | Execution Tuning，行级/指令级 trace 粒度训练 LLM，1.5M+ 程序，dynamic scratchpad 处理长 trace | 系统研究 trace FT 粒度与有效性，CruxEval ~80% 但下游代码生成收益有限 |
17	| 5 | **Do Code Semantics Help? (DCSH)** | EMNLP 2025 Findings | 5 种 trace 格式（Scratchpad/NExT/SemCoder/CodeExecutor/Concise）做 SFT，结论：trace 对 SFT 提升有限，对推理时 self-debugging/voting 更有价值 | 直接竞争——对"trace + SFT"方向的系统批判，负面结论恰好是我们的研究动机 |
18	| 6 | **CWM: Open-Weights LLM for Code Generation with World Models** | arXiv 2025 (Meta FAIR, 32B) | 常规预训练后 mid-training：Python interpreter trace + Docker agentic 轨迹混入（+5T tokens），再 SFT + RL | 工业级 trace mid-training 实践，验证方向被工业界采纳 |
19	| 7 | **Investigating Execution-Aware LMs for Code Optimization** | ICPC 2025 | CodeT5+ 三种训练策略 × 四种执行信息（行执行次数/行覆盖/分支覆盖/变量状态），代码优化任务 | 消融研究，结论偏负面（执行信息对代码优化提升有限） |
20	| 8 | **ET-CoT: Execution Trace Chain of Thought** | ICML 2025 Workshop | PyTracify 生成大规模 trace，SFT 微调 LLM 生成 trace CoT 预测程序输出 | CRUXEval-O 和 LiveCodeBench 上验证 |
21	
22	边缘相关（提示工程而非训练）：
23	- **Towards Effectively Leveraging Execution Traces for Program Repair** (arXiv 2025, JPMorgan): trace 加入 prompt，长 trace 反而降低效果
24	- **Runtime Traces Guided APR with Multi-Agent Debate** (arXiv 2026): 多智能体调用 trace，不涉及训练
25	- **EG-CFG: Execution Guided Line-by-Line Code Generation** (NeurIPS 2025): 推理时执行反馈，不做 trace training
26	
27	**结论：这是一个真实且活跃的研究方向（8 篇直接相关，2023-2025 年持续产出）。** 关键规律：(1) 多数工作聚焦"模拟执行"（predict output/trace），而非用 trace 提升分类；(2) DCSH 和 ICPC'25 均报告 SFT 收益有限，方向存在争议，恰好是研究空间；(3) Meta CWM 的工业投入验证了方向价值。
28	
29	---
30	
31	## 方向 2: Exception/Error/Fault 信息用于 LLM 训练
32	
33	### DCSH 的 5 种 trace 格式（确认）
34	
35	1. **Scratchpad** — LLM 做中间计算的草稿格式
36	2. **NExT** — 执行 trace 作为内联注释嵌入代码
37	3. **SemCoder** — 自然语言逐行描述执行过程
38	4. **CodeExecutor** — 记录每行变量状态变化
39	5. **Concise** — CodeExecutor 变体，忽略值未改变的变量
40	
41	**关键发现：5 种格式全部是完整语义执行信息（变量状态、逐行描述），不包含 exception-only 变体。没有人在 trace 格式消融中测试过 exception-only。**
42	
43	### 找到的相关工作
44	
45	| # | 标题 | 会议/年份 | 核心做法 | 与我们的关系 |
46	|---|------|----------|----------|-------------|
47	| 1 | **Fault Localization via Fine-tuning LLMs with Mutation Generated Stack Traces** | ICST 2025 Industry (Jambigi et al.) | 变异生成 64K 条 crash 数据，FT LLM，输入仅 stack trace（方法名+行号），预测 root cause 函数。SAP HANA 66.9% | **最接近的工作**——仅用 crash/exception 信号（无完整 trace），发现最小信号就够用。加入源代码反而降低准确率。但任务是 fault localization |
48	| 2 | **SBEST: Leveraging Stack Traces for Spectrum-based FL** | EMSE 2024 (Pacheco et al.) | 将 stack trace 整合进 SBFL。关键实证：**98.3% 的 bug 修复直接对应 stack trace 中的 exception** | "minimal diagnostic signal"的最强实证支撑——98.3% 相关性证明 exception 信号的充分性 |
49	| 3 | **LLM4PatchCorrect** | TSE 2024 (Zhou et al.) | ICL，输入 = bug description + execution trace + failing tests + coverage + similar patches，判断 patch correct/overfitting。StarCoder，84.4% acc | 做 binary verdict 但需要多种信号组合才达 84.4%，我们用 exception-only 达 99.83%，对比鲜明 |
50	| 4 | **AutoCrashFL** | arXiv 2025 (SAP) | LLM agent + crash dump + source code，工业规模 crash root cause 定位，30% acc@1 | 同样以 crash/exception 为核心输入，工业规模验证 |
51	| 5 | **Crash Report Enhancement with LLMs** | arXiv 2025 | LLM 增强 crash report（stack trace + repo 代码），bug 定位准确率 10.6% → 40.2-43.1% | stack trace/exception 作为核心输入信号的又一例证 |
52	
53	**结论：没有发现任何人专门用 exception-only 信号做 verdict prediction 并报告 99%+ 准确率。** ICST'25 的 stack trace FT 和 SBEST 的 98.3% 实证是最接近的支撑。fault localization 领域有"minimal diagnostic signal"的初步发现，但未迁移到 verdict/classification 任务。这是明确的文献空白。
54	
55	---
56	
57	## 方向 3: 信息粒度 / 任务复杂度匹配
58	
59	### 信息论核心文献
60	
61	| # | 标题 | 会议/年份 | 核心做法 | 与我们的关系 |
62	|---|------|----------|----------|-------------|
63	| 1 | **Deep Learning and the Information Bottleneck Principle** | IEEE ITW 2015 (Tishby & Zaslavsky) | DNN 每层是对输入的有损压缩，最优表示应最大化与标签的互信息同时最小化与输入的互信息 | **直接理论支撑**——IB 框架证明分类网络应压缩任务无关信息，exception-only 等价于手动执行此压缩 |
64	| 2 | **On the Information Bottleneck Theory of Deep Learning** | ICLR 2018 (Saxe et al.) | 反驳 Tishby 的"压缩普遍性"——压缩只在双饱和激活下出现；但确认输入含任务无关子集时表示会压缩无关信息 | 部分支撑——不能说"压缩所以更好"，应说"去掉任务无关噪声使信号更纯净" |
65	| 3 | **Minimal Achievable Sufficient Statistic (MASS) Learning** | ICML 2019 | 训练目标使网络学到"最小充分统计量"，引入 CDI 处理确定性变量 | **形式化支撑**——exception-only 是 pass/fail 任务的近似最小充分统计量 |
66	
67	### 任务导向压缩
68	
69	| # | 标题 | 会议/年份 | 核心做法 | 与我们的关系 |
70	|---|------|----------|----------|-------------|
71	| 4 | **Task-Oriented Lossy Compression with Classification Constraints** | IEEE ITW 2024 | 推导 binary/Gaussian 源的率-失真-分类闭合解，证明分类任务的最小充分信息量远小于重建任务 | **信息论量化支撑**——binary classification 只需极少信息，full trace 的信息率远超所需 |
72	
73	### LLM 长上下文性能退化
74	
75	| # | 标题 | 会议/年份 | 核心做法 | 与我们的关系 |
76	|---|------|----------|----------|-------------|
77	| 5 | **Lost in the Middle: How Language Models Use Long Contexts** | TACL 2023 (Liu et al.) | 多文档 QA 中信息在中间时性能低于闭卷基线，U 形曲线，30+ 文档时准确率降 30%+ | **最强实证类比**——full trace 把 exception 信号淹没在执行细节中，等价于相关信息在超长上下文中间 |
78	| 6 | **Context Length Alone Hurts LLM Performance Despite Perfect Retrieval** | arXiv 2025 | 控制变量：检索完美时仅增加上下文长度就导致 5 个模型性能降 13.9%-85%，即使无关 token 替换为空白仍下降 | **极强直接支撑**——full trace 的长度本身就是负担，exception-only 消除了长度惩罚 |
79

      "content": "# Trace-Verdict-Study 文献调研报告\n\n日期：2026-05-15\n\n---\n\n## 方向 1: Trace SFT / Trace-augmented LLM Training 全景\n\n除已知工作外，找到 8 篇直接相关工作：\n\n| # | 标题 | 会议/年份 | 核心做法 | 与我们的关系 |\n|---|------|----------|----------|-------------|\n| 1 | **SemCoder: Training Code Language Models with Comprehensive Semantics Reasoning** | NeurIPS 2024 | 训练 Code LLM 用自然语言逐行描述控制流和变量状态（\"execution monologue\"），在全量可执行 Python 上做 SFT | trace-as-text SFT 的代表性工作，关注\"模拟执行\"而非分类 |\n| 2 | **NExT: Teaching LLMs to Reason about Code Execution** | arXiv 2024 (Google DeepMind) | 自训练方法，将行级变量状态 trace 嵌入 CoT，SFT 微调 PaLM 2 做程序修复 | trace SFT 用于 repair，证明 trace-aware CoT 可泛化到推理时无 trace 场景 |\n| 3 | **CodeExecutor** | Findings of ACL 2023 | 构造 mutation-based Python 数据集（含逐行变量状态），课程学习做 execution trace pretraining | 最早的 trace pretraining 工作之一，让模型学会\"执行\"代码 |\n| 4 | **What I Cannot Execute, I Do Not Understand (E.T.)** | arXiv 2025 (Meta + Edinburgh) | Execution Tuning，行级/指令级 trace 粒度训练 LLM，1.5M+ 程序，dynamic scratchpad 处理长 trace | 系统研究 trace FT 粒度与有效性，CruxEval ~80% 但下游代码生成收益有限 |\n| 5 | **Do Code Semantics Help? (DCSH)** | EMNLP 2025 Findings | 5 种 trace 格式（Scratchpad/NExT/SemCoder/CodeExecutor/Concise）做 SFT，结论：trace 对 SFT 提升有限，对推理时 self-debugging/voting 更有价值 | 直接竞争——对\"trace + SFT\"方向的系统批判，负面结论恰好是我们的研究动机 |\n| 6 | **CWM: Open-Weights LLM for Code Generation with World Models** | arXiv 2025 (Meta FAIR, 32B) | 常规预训练后 mid-training：Python interpreter trace + Docker agentic 轨迹混入（+5T tokens），再 SFT + RL | 工业级 trace mid-training 实践，验证方向被工业界采纳 |\n| 7 | **Investigating Execution-Aware LMs for Code Optimization** | ICPC 2025 | CodeT5+ 三种训练策略 × 四种执行信息（行执行次数/行覆盖/分支覆盖/变量状态），代码优化任务 | 消融研究，结论偏负面（执行信息对代码优化提升有限） |\n| 8 | **ET-CoT: Execution Trace Chain of Thought** | ICML 2025 Workshop | PyTracify 生成大规模 trace，SFT 微调 LLM 生成 trace CoT 预测程序输出 | CRUXEval-O 和 LiveCodeBench 上验证 |\n\n边缘相关（提示工程而非训练）：\n- **Towards Effectively Leveraging Execution Traces for Program Repair** (arXiv 2025, JPMorgan): trace 加入 prompt，长 trace 反而降低效果\n- **Runtime Traces Guided APR with Multi-Agent Debate** (arXiv 2026): 多智能体调用 trace，不涉及训练\n- **EG-CFG: Execution Guided Line-by-Line Code Generation** (NeurIPS 2025): 推理时执行反馈，不做 trace training\n\n**结论：这是一个真实且活跃的研究方向（8 篇直接相关，2023-2025 年持续产出）。** 关键规律：(1) 多数工作聚焦\"模拟执行\"（predict output/trace），而非用 trace 提升分类；(2) DCSH 和 ICPC'25 均报告 SFT 收益有限，方向存在争议，恰好是研究空间；(3) Meta CWM 的工业投入验证了方向价值。\n\n---\n\n## 方向 2: Exception/Error/Fault 信息用于 LLM 训练\n\n### DCSH 的 5 种 trace 格式（确认）\n\n1. **Scratchpad** — LLM 做中间计算的草稿格式\n2. **NExT** — 执行 trace 作为内联注释嵌入代码\n3. **SemCoder** — 自然语言逐行描述执行过程\n4. **CodeExecutor** — 记录每行变量状态变化\n5. **Concise** — CodeExecutor 变体，忽略值未改变的变量\n\n**关键发现：5 种格式全部是完整语义执行信息（变量状态、逐行描述），不包含 exception-only 变体。没有人在 trace 格式消融中测试过 exception-only。**\n\n### 找到的相关工作\n\n| # | 标题 | 会议/年份 | 核心做法 | 与我们的关系 |\n|---|------|----------|----------|-------------|\n| 1 | **Fault Localization via Fine-tuning LLMs with Mutation Generated Stack Traces** | ICST 2025 Industry (Jambigi et al.) | 变异生成 64K 条 crash 数据，FT LLM，输入仅 stack trace（方法名+行号），预测 root cause 函数。SAP HANA 66.9% | **最接近的工作**——仅用 crash/exception 信号（无完整 trace），发现最小信号就够用。加入源代码反而降低准确率。但任务是 fault localization |\n| 2 | **SBEST: Leveraging Stack Traces for Spectrum-based FL** | EMSE 2024 (Pacheco et al.) | 将 stack trace 整合进 SBFL。关键实证：**98.3% 的 bug 修复直接对应 stack trace 中的 exception** | \"minimal diagnostic signal\"的最强实证支撑——98.3% 相关性证明 exception 信号的充分性 |\n| 3 | **LLM4PatchCorrect** | TSE 2024 (Zhou et al.) | ICL，输入 = bug description + execution trace + failing tests + coverage + similar patches，判断 patch correct/overfitting。StarCoder，84.4% acc | 做 binary verdict 但需要多种信号组合才达 84.4%，我们用 exception-only 达 99.83%，对比鲜明 |\n| 4 | **AutoCrashFL** | arXiv 2025 (SAP) | LLM agent + crash dump + source code，工业规模 crash root cause 定位，30% acc@1 | 同样以 crash/exception 为核心输入，工业规模验证 |\n| 5 | **Crash Report Enhancement with LLMs** | arXiv 2025 | LLM 增强 crash report（stack trace + repo 代码），bug 定位准确率 10.6% → 40.2-43.1% | stack trace/exception 作为核心输入信号的又一例证 |\n\n**结论：没有发现任何人专门用 exception-only 信号做 verdict prediction 并报告 99%+ 准确率。** ICST'25 的 stack trace FT 和 SBEST 的 98.3% 实证是最接近的支撑。fault localization 领域有\"minimal diagnostic signal\"的初步发现，但未迁移到 verdict/classification 任务。这是明确的文献空白。\n\n---\n\n## 方向 3: 信息粒度 / 任务复杂度匹配\n\n### 信息论核心文献\n\n| # | 标题 | 会议/年份 | 核心做法 | 与我们的关系 |\n|---|------|----------|----------|-------------|\n| 1 | **Deep Learning and the Information Bottleneck Principle** | IEEE ITW 2015 (Tishby & Zaslavsky) | DNN 每层是对输入的有损压缩，最优表示应最大化与标签的互信息同时最小化与输入的互信息 | **直接理论支撑**——IB 框架证明分类网络应压缩任务无关信息，exception-only 等价于手动执行此压缩 |\n| 2 | **On the Information Bottleneck Theory of Deep Learning** | ICLR 2018 (Saxe et al.) | 反驳 Tishby 的\"压缩普遍性\"——压缩只在双饱和激活下出现；但确认输入含任务无关子集时表示会压缩无关信息 | 部分支撑——不能说\"压缩所以更好\"，应说\"去掉任务无关噪声使信号更纯净\" |\n| 3 | **Minimal Achievable Sufficient Statistic (MASS) Learning** | ICML 2019 | 训练目标使网络学到\"最小充分统计量\"，引入 CDI 处理确定性变量 | **形式化支撑**——exception-only 是 pass/fail 任务的近似最小充分统计量 |\n\n### 任务导向压缩\n\n| # | 标题 | 会议/年份 | 核心做法 | 与我们的关系 |\n|---|------|----------|----------|-------------|\n| 4 | **Task-Oriented Lossy Compression with Classification Constraints** | IEEE ITW 2024 | 推导 binary/Gaussian 源的率-失真-分类闭合解，证明分类任务的最小充分信息量远小于重建任务 | **信息论量化支撑**——binary classification 只需极少信息，full trace 的信息率远超所需 |\n\n### LLM 长上下文性能退化\n\n| # | 标题 | 会议/年份 | 核心做法 | 与我们的关系 |\n|---|------|----------|----------|-------------|\n| 5 | **Lost in the Middle: How Language Models Use Long Contexts** | TACL 2023 (Liu et al.) | 多文档 QA 中信息在中间时性能低于闭卷基线，U 形曲线，30+ 文档时准确率降 30%+ | **最强实证类比**——full trace 把 exception 信号淹没在执行细节中，等价于相关信息在超长上下文中间 |\n| 6 | **Context Length Alone Hurts LLM Performance Despite Perfect Retrieval** | arXiv 2025 | 控制变量：检索完美时仅增加上下文长度就导致 5 个模型性能降 13.9%-85%，即使无关 token 替换为空白仍下降 | **极强直接支撑**——full trace 的长度本身就是负担，exception-only 消除了长度惩罚 |\n\n### 输入特征与标签粒度匹配",
      "numLines": 80,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 156
    }

Now I have the complete calibration picture. Let me compile the full results:

| Condition | Accuracy (calibration) | Accuracy (registry) | ECE | Brier | MCE |
|-----------|----------------------|---------------------|-----|-------|-----|
| ae_only | 99.916% | 99.92% | 0.0004 | 0.0008 | — (none, all in 1 bin) |
| exception_only | 99.83% | 99.83% | 0.0024 | 0.0013 | 0.836 |


| vota | 99.41% | 99.16% | 0.0054 | 0.0052 | 0.621 |
| loop_only | 85.63% | 85.63% | 0.0833 | 0.1183 | null |
| label_only | 86.22% | 86.22% | 0.1108 | 0.1202 | — |
| full_trace | 81.76% | 81.76% | 0.0946 | 0.1438 | 0.249 |
| no_trace | 75.13% | 75.1% | — | — | — |

The results show a clear hierarchy where the exception handling and voting approaches maintain excellent calibration, while the loop and label-only conditions drop significantly in accuracy and calibration quality. Looking at label_only's calibration, it's severely overconfident—ECE of 0.111 is worse than full_trace despite higher accuracy, with 86.4% of predictions crammed into the high-confidence bin but only 90.8% actually correct there. The [0.9, 0.95] bin is particularly bad with 51.2% accuracy despite 0.93 confidence, and fail predictions are catastrophically miscalibrated at ECE=0.270, which makes sense given that label_only training focuses entirely on the loss function without broader signal diversity.

I'm also noticing some data inconsistencies: VOTA shows 99.41% calibration versus 99.16% in the registry, and the registry itself is incomplete with only 4 conditions recorded while label_only and ae_only are missing entirely. The calibration metrics file was written before those conditions finished running.

So the key takeaway is that label_only trades calibration for a marginal accuracy gain over full_trace—it learns to be decisive but poorly calibrated, which is an important finding for the paper.

title: \\\"What I cannot execute, I do not understand: Training and Evaluating LLMs on Program Execution Traces\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Lorenzo Ferrando, Nicola Ferraro, et al.\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2503.05703\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"对比 regular/compact/dynamic 三种 scratchpad 策略，compact（仅记录变化变量）略优于 regular（全部变量值）。CruxEval/MBPP ~80%。直接支持 plan_001：完整值信息冗余，精简不损失效果。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          cruxeval_accuracy: \\\"~80%\\\"\\n          mbpp_accuracy: \\\"~80%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Demystifying Errors in LLM Reasoning Traces: An Empirical Study of Code Execution Simulation\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"arXiv 2512.00215\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv\\\"\\n        role: evaluation\\n        key_finding: \\\"LLM 在循环中频繁遗漏/多算变量修改，函数返回值预测不准。说明 LLM 对具体值追踪能力弱于结构理解，为 plan_001 发现（值冗余）提供机制解释。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n  code-verdict:\\n    description: \\\"LLM 判断代码 pass/fail、正确性分类、mutation testing\\\"\\n    papers:\\n      - title: \\\"APPT: Boosting Automated Patch Correctness Prediction via Fine-tuning Pre-trained Models\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"iSEngLab (Nanjing University)\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"IEEE TSE 2024\\\"\\n        role: direct-competitor\\n        key_finding: \\\"BERT + LSTM + classifier 微调做 patch 正确性二分类。在 1183 个 Defects4J patches 上 accuracy 79.7%、recall 83.2%，优于 CACHE。与 VOTA 的 verdict 分类目标最接近。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          accuracy: \\\"79.7%\\\"\\n          recall: \\\"83.2%\\\"\\n          f1_improvement_from_finetuning: \\\"+10.22%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Calibration and Correctness of Language Models for Code\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Claudio Spiess, David Gros, et al.\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"ICSE 2025\\\"\\n        role: evaluation\\n        key_finding: \\\"代码生成模型的置信度校准普遍较差。Platt scaling 可改善但需要先验正确性数据。对 VOTA 的启示：verdict 分类需关注校准，不只是准确率。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Mutation-Guided LLM-based Test Generation at Meta\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Christopher Foster et al. (Meta)\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"FSE 2025 (Industry)\\\"\\n        role: evaluation\\n        key_finding: \\\"LLM equivalence detector 判断 mutant 是否等价，加简单预处理后 precision 0.95、recall 0.96。大规模部署到 10K+ 类，工程师接受率 73%。展示了 LLM 做代码正确性判断的工业可行性。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          precision: \\\"0.95\\\"\\n          recall: \\\"0.96\\\"\\n          classes_evaluated: \\\"10795\\\"\\n          test_acceptance_rate: \\\"73%\\\"\\n\\n  trace-compression:\\n    description: \\\"Execution trace 的压缩、摘要、抽象方法\\\"\\n    papers:\\n      - title: \\\"Predictive Program Slicing via Execution Knowledge-Guided Dynamic Dependence Learning\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Aashish Yadavally, Yi Li, Tien Nguyen\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"FSE 2024\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"ND-Slicer 用 execution-aware 预训练学习动态依赖，无需实际执行即可预测特定输入的程序切片。Python 上 exact-match 81.31%、ROUGE-LCS 0.954。与 VOTA 的 assertion-point snapshots 思路类似——都是从完整 trace 中提取关键信息。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          exact_match: \\\"81.31%\\\"\\n          rouge_lcs: \\\"0.954\\\"\\n          fault_localization_accuracy: \\\"63.88%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Towards Effectively Leveraging Execution Traces for Program Repair with Code LLMs\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Mirazul Haque, Petr Babkin, et al. (J.P. Morgan AI Research)\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"KnowledgeNLP Workshop @ ACL 2025\\\"\\n        role: background\\n        key_finding: \\\"直接将 execution trace 加入 prompt 帮助有限，且随 trace 复杂度增加效果递减。LLM 优化的 prompt 格式比原始 trace 更一致地优于无 trace baseline。直接支持 VOTA 的核心假设：原始 trace 太冗长，需要专门的压缩/抽象。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Making LLMs Program Interpreters via Execution Trace Chain of Thought (ET-CoT)\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"ICML 2025 Workshop PRAL\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"ICML 2025 Workshop\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"开发 PyTracify 工具生成 sub-line 粒度的结构化 execution trace，SFT 后 Qwen2.5-7B 在 CRUXEval-O 达 70.0%、LiveCodeBench 达 88.3%，超过同规模 code-oriented 模型。其 trace 格式本身是一种 trace abstraction。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          cruxeval_o: \\\"70.0%\\\"\\n          livecodebench_execution: \\\"88.3%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"TraceCoder: A Trace-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Debugging of LLM-Generated Code\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"ICSE 2026 Research Track\\\"\\n        year: 2026\\n        venue: \\\"ICSE 2026\\\"\\n        role: background\\n        key_finding: \\\"在特定位置插入 diagnostic probes 而非全量 trace 记录，Pass@1 提升 34.43%。选择性 trace 采集优于全量，支持 VOTA 的行为模式抽象思路。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          pass_at_1_improvement: \\\"+34.43%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Runtime Execution Traces Guided Automated Program Repair with Multi-Agent Debate\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Jiaqing Wu et al.\\\"\\n        year: 2026\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2604.02647\\\"\\n        role: background\\n        key_finding: \\\"Probe agent 选择性捕获 critical variables 的 execution snapshots 用于 multi-agent repair debate，修复 392 个缺陷。验证了选择性 trace 信息优于全量记录。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          defects_fixed: \\\"392\\\"\\n\\n  sft-classification:\\n    description: \\\"用 SFT 做分类任务的方法论和 findings\\\"\\n    papers:\\n      - title: \\\"Fine-Tuning Causal LLMs for Text Classification: Embedding-Based vs. Instruction-Based Approaches\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Amirhossein Yousefiramandi, Ciaran Cooney\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2512.12677\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"对比 embedding-based（加分类头）vs instruction-based（prompt->response）两种 SFT 分类方式。Embedding-based + QLoRA 在 F1 上与 instruction-tuned 持平，但可训练参数少得多。对 VOTA 的 verdict 分类有直接参考价值——选哪种 SFT 格式。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"FisherSFT: Data-Efficient Supervised Fine-Tuning of Language Models Using Information Gain\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Rohan Deb, Kiran Koshy Thekumparampil, et al.\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"ICML 2025\\\"\\n        role: background\\n        key_finding: \\\"用 Fisher 信息矩阵选择信息量最大的训练样本子集，通过 last-layer 线性化高效近似。在固定数据预算下提升 SFT 效果。对 VOTA 的数据选择策略有参考价值。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Fine-Tuned 'Small' LLMs (Still) Significantly Outperform Zero-Shot Generative AI Models in Text Classification\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Martin Juan Jose Bucher, Marco Martini\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2406.08660\\\"\\n        role: background\\n        key_finding: \\\"跨多种分类任务（情感、立场、情绪等），fine-tuned 小模型全面优于 GPT-4/Claude 的 zero-shot。验证了 VOTA 用 SFT 做 verdict 分类的基本假设——fine-tuning 在分类上优于 prompting。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\nmeta:\\n  last_updated: \\\"2026-05-15\\\"\\n  total_papers: 16\\n  search_scope: \\\"2024-2026, top ML/NLP/SE venues\\\"\\n  key_gaps:\\n    - title: \\\"Code Execution as Grounded Supervision for LLM Reasoning\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Dongwon Jung, Wenxuan Zhou, Muhao Chen\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"EMNLP 2025\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"用 Python debugger (Snoop) 提取 step-by-step execution trace，转换为自然语言 CoT。训练后模型推理能力提升且生成更少 token（减少 overthinking），具备跨域迁移能力。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"What I cannot execute, I do not understand: Training and Evaluating LLMs on Program Execution Traces\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Lorenzo Ferrando, Nicola Ferraro, et al.\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2503.05703\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"对比 regular/compact/dynamic 三种 scratchpad 策略，compact（仅记录变化变量）略优于 regular（全部变量值）。CruxEval/MBPP ~80%。直接支持 plan_001：完整值信息冗余，精简不损失效果。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          cruxeval_accuracy: \\\"~80%\\\"\\n          mbpp_accuracy: \\\"~80%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Demystifying Errors in LLM Reasoning Traces: An Empirical Study of Code Execution Simulation\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"arXiv 2512.00215\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv\\\"\\n        role: evaluation\\n        key_finding: \\\"LLM 在循环中频繁遗漏/多算变量修改，函数返回值预测不准。说明 LLM 对具体值追踪能力弱于结构理解，为 plan_001 发现（值冗余）提供机制解释。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n  code-verdict:\\n    description: \\\"LLM 判断代码 pass/fail、正确性分类、mutation testing\\\"\\n    papers:\\n      - title: \\\"APPT: Boosting Automated Patch Correctness Prediction via Fine-tuning Pre-trained Models\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"iSEngLab (Nanjing University)\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"IEEE TSE 2024\\\"\\n        role: direct-competitor\\n        key_finding: \\\"BERT + LSTM + classifier 微调做 patch 正确性二分类。在 1183 个 Defects4J patches 上 accuracy 79.7%、recall 83.2%，优于 CACHE。与 VOTA 的 verdict 分类目标最接近。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          accuracy: \\\"79.7%\\\"\\n          recall: \\\"83.2%\\\"\\n          f1_improvement_from_finetuning: \\\"+10.22%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Calibration and Correctness of Language Models for Code\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Claudio Spiess, David Gros, et al.\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"ICSE 2025\\\"\\n        role: evaluation\\n        key_finding: \\\"代码生成模型的置信度校准普遍较差。Platt scaling 可改善但需要先验正确性数据。对 VOTA 的启示：verdict 分类需关注校准，不只是准确率。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Mutation-Guided LLM-based Test Generation at Meta\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Christopher Foster et al. (Meta)\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"FSE 2025 (Industry)\\\"\\n        role: evaluation\\n        key_finding: \\\"LLM equivalence detector 判断 mutant 是否等价，加简单预处理后 precision 0.95、recall 0.96。大规模部署到 10K+ 类，工程师接受率 73%。展示了 LLM 做代码正确性判断的工业可行性。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          precision: \\\"0.95\\\"\\n          recall: \\\"0.96\\\"\\n          classes_evaluated: \\\"10795\\\"\\n          test_acceptance_rate: \\\"73%\\\"\\n\\n  trace-compression:\\n    description: \\\"Execution trace 的压缩、摘要、抽象方法\\\"\\n    papers:\\n      - title: \\\"Predictive Program Slicing via Execution Knowledge-Guided Dynamic Dependence Learning\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Aashish Yadavally, Yi Li, Tien Nguyen\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"FSE 2024\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"ND-Slicer 用 execution-aware 预训练学习动态依赖，无需实际执行即可预测特定输入的程序切片。Python 上 exact-match 81.31%、ROUGE-LCS 0.954。与 VOTA 的 assertion-point snapshots 思路类似——都是从完整 trace 中提取关键信息。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          exact_match: \\\"81.31%\\\"\\n          rouge_lcs: \\\"0.954\\\"\\n          fault_localization_accuracy: \\\"63.88%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Towards Effectively Leveraging Execution Traces for Program Repair with Code LLMs\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Mirazul Haque, Petr Babkin, et al. (J.P. Morgan AI Research)\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"KnowledgeNLP Workshop @ ACL 2025\\\"\\n        role: background\\n        key_finding: \\\"直接将 execution trace 加入 prompt 帮助有限，且随 trace 复杂度增加效果递减。LLM 优化的 prompt 格式比原始 trace 更一致地优于无 trace baseline。直接支持 VOTA 的核心假设：原始 trace 太冗长，需要专门的压缩/抽象。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Making LLMs Program Interpreters via Execution Trace Chain of Thought (ET-CoT)\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"ICML 2025 Workshop PRAL\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"ICML 2025 Workshop\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"开发 PyTracify 工具生成 sub-line 粒度的结构化 execution trace，SFT 后 Qwen2.5-7B 在 CRUXEval-O 达 70.0%、LiveCodeBench 达 88.3%，超过同规模 code-oriented 模型。其 trace 格式本身是一种 trace abstraction。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          cruxeval_o: \\\"70.0%\\\"\\n          livecodebench_execution: \\\"88.3%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"TraceCoder: A Trace-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Debugging of LLM-Generated Code\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"ICSE 2026 Research Track\\\"\\n        year: 2026\\n        venue: \\\"ICSE 2026\\\"\\n        role: background\\n        key_finding: \\\"在特定位置插入 diagnostic probes 而非全量 trace 记录，Pass@1 提升 34.43%。选择性 trace 采集优于全量，支持 VOTA 的行为模式抽象思路。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          pass_at_1_improvement: \\\"+34.43%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Runtime Execution Traces Guided Automated Program Repair with Multi-Agent Debate\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Jiaqing Wu et al.\\\"\\n        year: 2026\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2604.02647\\\"\\n        role: background\\n        key_finding: \\\"Probe agent 选择性捕获 critical variables 的 execution snapshots 用于 multi-agent repair debate，修复 392 个缺陷。验证了选择性 trace 信息优于全量记录。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          defects_fixed: \\\"392\\\"\\n\\n  sft-classification:\\n    description: \\\"用 SFT 做分类任务的方法论和 findings\\\"\\n    papers:\\n      - title: \\\"Fine-Tuning Causal LLMs for Text Classification: Embedding-Based vs. Instruction-Based Approaches\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Amirhossein Yousefiramandi, Ciaran Cooney\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2512.12677\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"对比 embedding-based（加分类头）vs instruction-based（prompt->response）两种 SFT 分类方式。Embedding-based + QLoRA 在 F1 上与 instruction-tuned 持平，但可训练参数少得多。对 VOTA 的 verdict 分类有直接参考价值——选哪种 SFT 格式。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"FisherSFT: Data-Efficient Supervised Fine-Tuning of Language Models Using Information Gain\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Rohan Deb, Kiran Koshy Thekumparampil, et al.\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"ICML 2025\\\"\\n        role: background\\n        key_finding: \\\"用 Fisher 信息矩阵选择信息量最大的训练样本子集，通过 last-layer 线性化高效近似。在固定数据预算下提升 SFT 效果。对 VOTA 的数据选择策略有参考价值。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Fine-Tuned 'Small' LLMs (Still) Significantly Outperform Zero-Shot Generative AI Models in Text Classification\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Martin Juan Jose Bucher, Marco Martini\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2406.08660\\\"\\n        role: background\\n        key_finding: \\\"跨多种分类任务（情感、立场、情绪等），fine-tuned 小模型全面优于 GPT-4/Claude 的 zero-shot。验证了 VOTA 用 SFT 做 verdict 分类的基本假设——fine-tuning 在分类上优于 prompting。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\nmeta:\\n  last_updated: \\\"2026-05-15\\\"\\n  total_papers: 16\\n  search_scope: \\\"2024-2026, top ML/NLP/SE venues\\\"\\n  key_gaps:\\n    - ```text
The literature_map.yaml still doesn't include TRACED (ICSE 2024) and TracePile (NeurIPS 2025). These are mentioned in paper_framing.md as key related works:

- TRACED (ICSE 2024): "showed traces help encoder-based classification"
- TracePile (NeurIPS 2025): "scaled to 2.6M samples but only evaluated generation"

The literature survey artifact covers them implicitly (DCSH is listed, TracePile and TRACED are discussed in context), but they're not explicitly added to literature_map.yaml. 

The survey found SemCoder, CodeExecutor, CWM, ICPC'25 work, SBEST, etc., but TRACED and TracePile are still not in the map.


I'm checking whether these papers might already be in the map under different names or descriptions. The "What I cannot execute, I do not understand" paper from 2025 could potentially be related, but the arXiv identifier doesn't match what I'd expect for a NeurIPS 2025 paper, so TracePile and TRACED appear to be distinct works that genuinely need to be added.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
ae_only pairwise bootstrap results are in. Let me verify these against the registry and the actual output file.

Key findings:
1. **ae_only vs no_trace**: +24.79pp [22.35, 27.31], p<0.0001 — highly significant, expected
2. **ae_only vs exception_only**: +0.08pp [-0.17, +0.34], p=0.80 — NOT significant. This confirms D013: ae_only ≈ exception_only, both Tier 1


3. **ae_only vs vota**: +0.50pp [0.17, 0.92], p=0.005 — SIGNIFICANT. ae_only is actually better than full VOTA

This is striking: the single assertion-point component (99.92%) outperforms the complete VOTA trace that combines exception, loop, and assertion-point components (99.16%). I need to check which VOTA accuracy figure was used here—the calibration showed 99.41% while the registry eval showed 99.16%, and the +0.50pp difference would put ae_only at 99.92%.

Looking at the bootstrap comparison, it appears to be using actual predictions rather than just reported numbers, which means this is a proper paired test. The +0.50pp margin suggests VOTA had 7 errors under the calibration method rather than 10 under text matching. The real takeaway is that adding the exception and loop components to assertion-point actually degrades performance slightly, which hints that those additional components might be introducing noise rather than signal.
```

### Assistant

_source line 739 · 2026-05-15T12:42:28.867Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
OOM error on GPU 0. The vota_seed123 experiment tried to run on cuda:0 but OOM'd because PID 93494 (deepseek_vota training) is using 21.86 GB there. The seed robustness experiment tried to allocate 4.64 GB on top of the existing usage and ran out of memory.

Key details:
- GPU 0 total: 94.97 GB
- PID 93494 (deepseek_vota): 21.86 GB in use
- vota_seed123 process: 24.48 GB in use (including non-PyTorch)


- Only 614.25 MB free when the allocation was attempted

The memory accounting seems off at first glance—nvidia-smi showed 71.5 GB used earlier, but the two processes account for less than that. The real problem is straightforward though: I launched vota_seed123 on cuda:0 while deepseek_vota_seed42 was already training there, so two training jobs collided on the same GPU and exhausted available memory.

The registry hasn't marked vota_seed123 as FAILED yet, which could mean the cron hook caught the failure before the registry updated, similar to the ae_only false positive pattern we saw before—except this time it's a genuine OOM error. I should check the registry to see the status of the seed robustness experiments.
```

</details>

```text
Critical findings:

1. **vota_seed123 status is DONE in registry** but the log shows OOM failure with EXIT_CODE=1. This is a **serious data integrity issue** — the experiment crashed but was marked DONE.

2. The spec shows `--gpu 0` for vota_seed123, which was already occupied by deepseek_vota_seed42 (PID 93494). This caused the OOM.

3. Currently only PID 93494 (deepseek_vota) is on GPU, meaning the vota_seed123 process has died (as expected from OOM).
   
4. vota_seed456 is PREPARING and likely targets the same GPU, which would trigger an identical collision.

The core issue is that the Agent marked a crashed experiment as DONE despite the OOM failure, scheduled conflicting GPU resources, and could end up citing non-existent results. I need to check whether the registry actually contains any metrics for vota_seed123 — if it's empty despite being marked DONE, that would confirm the Agent detected the failure but didn't properly handle it.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Important findings from the bootstrap pairwise file:

1. **Accuracies used**: ae_only=0.9992, exception_only=0.9983, no_trace=0.7513, vota=0.9941

The VOTA accuracy used is 0.9941 (99.41%), which is the calibration/logit-argmax accuracy, NOT the registry eval accuracy of 0.9916 (99.16%). This means the bootstrap comparison loaded VOTA predictions using the same method as ae_only (token logit argmax), not the text-matching method from evaluate.py.

This is **consistent within the comparison** (both ae_only and vota use the same prediction extraction method), but it uses the higher VOTA accuracy (99.41% instead of 99.16%). The diff of +0.50pp is therefore comparing like-for-like: both use logit argmax.
 
Now I'm checking what evaluate.py reports for ae_only—the registry shows 0.9992 and calibration also gives 0.99916, so ae_only is consistent across both methods. VOTA is the only one with a discrepancy between the two evaluation approaches. The key statistical findings hold: ae_only and exception_only are not significantly different (p=0.80, +0.08pp), confirming they're equivalent Tier 1 signals, while ae_only versus VOTA shows a significant difference (p=0.005, +0.50pp).

What's striking is that ae_only actually outperforms the full VOTA model—since VOTA combines exception, loop, and ae together, but ae_only (99.92%) beats VOTA full (99.41%), it suggests that adding loop compression and exception handling to ae_only degrades performance slightly. Even though exception_only (99.83%) and ae_only (99.92%) are both stronger than VOTA full, combining Tier 1 signals with Tier 2 (loop) appears to dilute the overall performance, which aligns with the gradient dilution hypothesis—adding more trace information, even when individually useful, can hurt when the core signal is already sufficiently strong.

I should verify whether the new registry entry is vota_seed123, which looks like a separate experiment rather than the ae_only update, so I need to check the ae_only registry entry specifically.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
OK, let me re-read this carefully. The registry actually shows:
- `status: "DONE"` but `conclusion: "failed"` 
- `failure_reason: "CUDA OOM: ..."`

So the registry IS marking it as a failure via `conclusion: "failed"`, though the status says "DONE" (meaning the experiment run is complete, not that it succeeded). This is the expected pattern — DONE means "finished running", and conclusion tells you whether it succeeded or failed.

Now let me look at the critical issue: **the spec says `--gpu 0` but the registry says `gpu: "cuda:1"` and `reproduce.gpu: "cuda:1"`**. 

The Agent allocated cuda:1 but the training command hardcoded `--gpu 0`, creating a resource conflict where the process tried to use the wrong GPU while deepseek_vota was already occupying cuda:0, leading to the OOM error. The mismatch between the registry's gpu allocation and the command's hardcoded flag suggests the Agent didn't properly override the original script's GPU specification. I need to escalate this immediately—the Agent fixed the registry entry to cuda:1 but left the actual command with --gpu 0, which will cause an out-of-memory collision when vota_seed456 starts running on the same GPU.
```

Now I see the full picture:

1. The script `run_seed_robustness.sh` defaults to `GPU=0` but accepts `--gpu` parameter
2. The Agent submitted vota_seed123 with the inline command that hardcodes `--gpu 0` (copied from the script default)
3. The registry entry shows `gpu: "cuda:1"` — the Agent WANTED to use cuda:1 but the command still says `--gpu 0`
4. This is a classic command-allocation mismatch

The script itself is fine (it accepts --gpu parameter), but the Agent generated the inline command with `--gpu 0` hardcoded instead of using `--gpu 1` to match the allocation.


I need to flag this to Director as a critical issue — the command isn't aligned with the GPU allocation, and vota_seed456 is likely affected the same way since it's still in PREPARING state.

The diagnosis worker confirms what I found: vota_seed123 was launched with `--gpu 0` instead of `--gpu 1`, causing OOM collision with deepseek_vota on cuda:0. cuda:1 is free.

The root cause is clear: the Agent's command used `--gpu 0` despite allocating `gpu: "cuda:1"` in the registry. This is a command-allocation mismatch.

Now I need to:
1. Verify the registry has been updated correctly (vota_seed123 marked as failed)


2. Check if vota_seed456 has the same --gpu 0 problem in its spec
3. Post to Director about the root cause and the need to fix the command before retrying

Let me look at vota_seed456's spec to see if it has the same issue.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/bin/bash\\n# Seed robustness experiment: train + eval VOTA condition with seeds 42/123/456.\\n# Verifies that VOTA 99.2% accuracy is stable across random seeds.\\n#\\n# GPU 参数必须与 registry 分配一致，不要假设默认值。\\n# 使用 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 隔离物理 GPU，训练/评估内部统一用 --gpu 0。\\nset -euo pipefail\\n\\nusage() {\\n    echo \\\"Usage: $0 [--gpu GPU_ID] [--seeds \\\\\\\"42 123 456\\\\\\\"] [--condition vota]\\\"\\n    exit 0\\n}\\n\\nGPU=1\\nSEEDS=\\\"42 123 456\\\"\\nCONDITION=\\\"vota\\\"\\n\\nwhile [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do\\n    case $1 in\\n        --gpu) GPU=\\\"$2\\\"; shift 2 ;;\\n        --seeds) SEEDS=\\\"$2\\\"; shift 2 ;;\\n        --condition) CONDITION=\\\"$2\\\"; shift 2 ;;\\n        -h|--help) usage ;;\\n        *) echo \\\"Unknown option: $1\\\"; exit 1 ;;\\n    esac\\ndone\\n\\n# --- GPU 可用性检查 ---\\nGPU_MEM_USED=$(nvidia-smi --id=\\\"$GPU\\\" --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits 2>/dev/null || echo \\\"ERROR\\\")\\nif [[ \\\"$GPU_MEM_USED\\\" == \\\"ERROR\\\" ]]; then\\n    echo \\\"ERROR: Cannot query GPU ${GPU}. Check nvidia-smi.\\\" >&2\\n    exit 1\\nfi\\nif [[ \\\"$GPU_MEM_USED\\\" -gt 1000 ]]; then\\n    echo \\\"ERROR: GPU ${GPU} already has ${GPU_MEM_USED} MiB in use (> 1000 MiB). Aborting to avoid OOM.\\\" >&2\\n    exit 1\\nfi\\n\\n# --- CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 隔离 ---\\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=$GPU\\necho \\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=${CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES} (physical GPU ${GPU})\\\"\\n\\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\n\\nCKPT_BASE=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints\\\"\\nPROJECT_DIR=\\\"/root/trace-verdict-study\\\"\\n\\necho \\\"=== Seed Robustness Experiment ===\\\"\\necho \\\"Condition: ${CONDITION}  |  Seeds: ${SEEDS}  |  GPU: ${GPU} (CVD isolated)\\\"\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.29}}"}

Interesting. Looking at the latest vota_seed123 spec:

```
command: "bash -c \"export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 && ... --gpu 0 ..."
gpu: ["cuda:1"]
pid: 105189
submitted_at: 2026-05-15T12:56:41
```

The Agent tried to fix it by adding `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1` at the beginning of the command, with `--gpu 0` (which would be device 0 within the CVD scope = physical GPU 1). But the failure_reason still shows the same OOM error about GPU 0 with Process 93494.
 
So if `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1` was actually set, then `--gpu 0` should map to physical GPU 1 and there shouldn't be any conflict with PID 93494 on physical GPU 0. Yet the error message is reporting GPU 0 with 94.97 GiB total capacity and Process 93494 holding 21.86 GiB — which means the process is somehow seeing physical GPU 0 instead of GPU 1. The `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1` export might not have taken effect, possibly due to a bash subshell issue or conda activate resetting the environment variable.

Looking at the actual command structure with `bash -c "export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 && source ... && conda activate base && ..."`, the export should happen before conda and Python run. But the failure_reason text is identical to the first OOM error down to the exact numbers — same GPU capacity, same process memory usage — which suggests either the failure_reason wasn't updated from the first failure, or the process really is still on physical GPU 0. Let me check the log file's updated_at timestamp to see if it's actually been refreshed.

The updated_at is 12:59:10 while submitted_at is 12:56:41, so it ran for about 2.5 minutes with a different PID (105189 vs 103055), meaning this is genuinely a new execution. If CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 was properly set and it still OOM'd on GPU 0, there might be an issue with environment variable propagation — conda activate is known to sometimes reset CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. I should check the actual log output rather than relying on the registry's failure_reason field. Since deepseek_vota is at 443/444 steps and should be finishing soon, the best move is to wait for that to complete first.

I'll just note this as a re-notification of the same issue without escalating again.

_source line 837 · 2026-05-15T13:00:11.077Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
OK so:

1. vota_seed123 OOM log is identical to first attempt — GPU 0, Process 93494. The `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1` didn't take effect. The error still says "GPU 0 has a total capacity of 94.97 GiB" which means the process was seeing physical GPU 0, not GPU 1.

Why didn't CVD work? Looking at the command:
```
bash -c "export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && ... 
python train/train_sft.py --gpu 0 ..."
```

This should work — CVD is set before conda activate. But there could be an issue if the train_sft.py script internally sets CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES or if `conda activate base` overrides it (some conda environments have activation scripts that reset GPU env vars). The error message shows 94.97 GiB which is physical GPU 0's capacity, not GPU 1's 97.9 GB, so the environment variable either wasn't propagated or got overridden somewhere in the activation chain. So the issue might be that `train_sft.py` itself is resetting `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES` internally — that's a common pattern in training scripts that would completely override the CVD=1 isolation set in the shell script. I should check if the training script is doing this, though I can't examine the full file without running out of context.
````

### Assistant

_source line 841 · 2026-05-15T13:00:26.888Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
Found it! Line 89 of train_sft.py:
```python
os.environ["CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"] = str(args.gpu)
```

When the command has `--gpu 0`, this sets `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0`, overriding the outer `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1`. So the process ends up on physical GPU 0, causing the OOM collision.

The fix worker's approach (CVD isolation + `--gpu 0`) would work IF train_sft.py didn't override CVD. But it does override CVD at line 89.


So I have three options: remove that CVD override entirely and let the outer environment variable control placement, pass `--gpu 1` directly to target the second GPU, or modify train_sft.py to check for an existing CVD setting first. The simplest path forward is just passing `--gpu 1` when we want cuda:1, though ideally either that line 89 gets removed or the wrapper script passes the correct physical GPU ID.

The Agent's solution missed this entirely—it didn't realize train_sft.py was overriding the CVD environment variable, so I need to flag that. Looking at the deepseek_vota logs, there's a new progress bar showing 190/298 steps, which appears to be the evaluation phase running after training completed at 443/444 steps.
````

</details>
